+| **Disable rule** | Remove rule (if source remains) | Disabling participation is reversible; archival source preserved |
+| **Continue writing** | Auto-complete book | Only insertion-point proposal is in MVP |
+| **Rewrite selection** | Rewrite chapter | One selected same-paragraph span only |
+| **Review suggestion** | Applied successfully (before author action) | AI response is provisional |
+| **Unsaved changes** | Saved (when pending) | Must reflect durability evidence |
+| **Save status unknown** | Conflict / Save failed | A lost ACK is not proof of either |
+| **Download manuscript** | Download complete backup | Single document export does not preserve all app state |
+| **Download book backup** | Publish book | Recovery archive is not distribution output |
+| **Sign out** | Delete session everywhere | Current security model cannot promise instant global token invalidation |
+| **Possible continuity issue** | AI detected bad writing | Context, genre and author intent matter |
+
+## 15. Agent instructions and integration rules
+
+### 15.1 Mandatory pre-change checklist
+
+For any task touching navigation, visible copy, UI states, status mapping, onboarding, accessibility text, dialogs, toasts, emails, files named for users or provider-consent wording:
+
+1. Read `AGENTS.md`, `PROJECT-CONSTITUTION.md`, `SOURCE-OF-TRUTH.md`, current `CURRENT-STATE.md`, the affected accepted scope/spec/ADR, and this document's relevant sections.
+2. Identify whether the feature is `EXISTING_UI`, approved but not implemented, proposed or deferred. Inspect actual component/source and API/error/DB state semantics **before** choosing words.
+3. Reuse preferred terms precisely; determine whether this is the same concept, a contextual abbreviation or a distinct domain object.
+4. Build a small copy mapping: surface/path, existing copy, proposed copy, semantic condition, expected action/result, accessibility name, associated test.
+5. Flag unknown terminology or conflict as `PENDING_DECISION`; do not rewrite product names, owner decisions, immutable fiction-guidance records or technical schemas independently.
+6. If wording implies a behavioral guarantee, identify the test/receipt/authorization evidence. If unverified, use qualified truthful wording or block the claim.
+7. Implement only within an owner-authorized coding task and existing Codex ContextPack/ExecutionBundle workflow. **This document does not authorize implementation.**
+8. Include copy and state coverage in tests and review evidence; update this record through separate owner-reviewed change control when adopting a new canonical term.
+
+### 15.2 Do not change without explicit approval
+
+- Approved M1-M8 scope, eleven journey IDs, the selected AI boundary and BYOK semantics.
+- Product name/trademark claims, major module names such as **Rules Studio** and **Story Profiles**, or default source-language content that would alter authored rules.
+- R01 compact/expanded frozen sources, their provenance IDs and owner mapping decisions.
+- Internal SQL/table/column names (`owner_id`, `accepted_document`, `chapter_revisions`), API routes (`/v1/books`), typed enums/response codes (`acknowledgment_unknown`, `session_changed`), TypeScript symbols, provider names, contractual record IDs, or migration history **solely to align with UI labels**.
+- Security, deletion, retention, cost, consent, export fidelity or save guarantees without appropriate verified implementation and owner approval.
+- An unimplemented future feature's navigation/CTA as if it worked.
+
+### 15.3 Enforcement and automated checks
+
+Human review is primary for semantics; regex cannot decide whether **Accept** and **Approve** mean the same thing in a new context. Pragmatic checks, when separately authorized, can:
+
+- inventory literals in `apps/web/src` (JSX text, attributes, `aria-label`, errors and constants) and compare against this vocabulary;
+- flag known high-risk display phrases (`Saved` without receipt context; `Save failed` on unknown ACK; `Canon updated` before approval; `free AI` with no verified basis);
+- test consent disclosure and actual transmitted context, including disabled rule exclusion;
+- test exact sign-in/library/empty/error states, retry uncertainty and duplicate risk;
+- test AI suggestion accept/reject/stale behavior and fact approval independence;
+- test export/backup and destructive confirmation distinctions;
+- run keyboard/screen-reader checks alongside visual copy checks.
+
+Do not automatically replace all instances of "create", "accept", "version", "story", "rule" or "save": many contextual uses are legitimate. A string-check suite cannot replace evidence of underlying authorization and data durability. Any new enforcement job must follow the project's CI/task governance and owner authorization.
+
+### 15.4 Exact `AGENTS.md` insertion candidate
+
+Originally proposed for insertion after `## Read in order` and before the operational workflow. **PR #77 now includes this snippet in `AGENTS.md` on its review branch; it is not yet merged into `main`.** This integration does not approve proposed product labels or alter existing owner/Codex authority.
+
+```markdown
+## Product language and UX copy (LANGUAGE.md)
+
+- Before any change to user-facing navigation, page names, buttons, forms, dialogs, status messages, errors, tooltips, accessibility labels, onboarding, AI consent or export wording, read root `LANGUAGE.md` alongside the relevant accepted product requirements and current implementation.
+- Follow `LANGUAGE.md` for owner-approved product terminology. Reuse canonical names consistently while allowing explicitly documented contextual short labels; preserve distinct concepts such as manuscript vs notes, AI suggestion vs accepted revision, suggested fact vs approved canon, and local recovery vs confirmed save.
+- Verify what the interface and backend actually do before using success, save, privacy, cost, cancellation, deletion or provider-availability claims. Do not expose internal transport codes as author-facing text or mark proposed/future features as implemented.
+- If a needed term is absent, proposed, disputed or inconsistent with approved scope, record the current wording, candidate, affected surfaces and supporting evidence for owner review. Do not silently make a new canonical decision or rename unrelated concepts.
+- `LANGUAGE.md` governs visible words, not database fields, API routes, internal identifiers, source archives, accepted ADRs, or integration contracts. Do not change those solely to match display labels.
+- Include user-facing copy/state checks and relevant accessibility/negative tests in the task ExecutionBundle. Do not change the language contract or add automation enforcing it outside the owner-authorized Task  ContextPack  implementation  review workflow.
+```
+
+## 16. Maintenance, approval and change control
+
+### 16.1 Adoption
+
+`LANGUAGE.md` currently has **proposed-owner-review** status. For it to become an effective source of truth: project owner reviews all `PENDING_DECISION` high-impact questions and accepts the vocabulary scope; implementation agent integrates document through an authorized task/PR as required by `AGENTS.md`; owner approves any updates to `AGENTS.md`; actual merged-main SHA and current state are reconciled. An unmerged local download or an AI-generated draft is not owner acceptance.
+
+### 16.2 Introducing or revising a term
+
+For each requested new/revised canonical term, capture:
+
+| Field | Record |
+|---|---|
+| Term ID | Stable `VEL-LANG-TERM-###` or review ID, assigned during adoption |
+| Concept and definition | Exact entity/action/state and its differences from adjacent terms |
+| Proposed UI wording | Full, short and contextual labels; forbidden confusion |
+| Classification | `EXISTING_UI`, `APPROVED_CONCEPT`, `PROPOSED_LABEL`, `PENDING_DECISION`, `FUTURE_RESERVED` |
+| Affected surfaces | UI paths, components, errors, docs, tests, accessibility names |
+| Evidence | Accepted scope/ADR, actual code, journeys, research and relevant source lines |
+| Decision | Owner approval/rejection/date/PR/commit; not implicit |
+| Transition | Where old label remains valid, replacement plan, old links/help compatibility |
+| Validation | Manual task + automated/accessible copy checks and verified states |
+
+Update terms through a reviewed versioned document change. Record superseded terms; do not simply erase historical source and variant identity. Maintain a small change log below as decisions occur.
+
+### 16.3 Release and review checklist
+
+- [ ] All enabled controls correspond to implemented behaviors; no phantom links.
+- [ ] Same concepts have the same primary name; differences are intentionally contextual.
+- [ ] Book / chapter / scene / notes / manuscript / series semantics preserved.
+- [ ] AI suggestion acceptance and story-fact approval cannot be mistaken for each other.
+- [ ] Local drafts, server save, unknown acknowledgement and true conflict have distinct messages.
+- [ ] No unreviewed rule source collision or false default-pack completeness claim.
+- [ ] No implied mandatory AI/provider spend or secret-persistence claim.
+- [ ] Forms have explicit labels, useful validation and accessible error feedback.
+- [ ] Deletion, export, backup, recovery and Undo explain actual effects and limits.
+- [ ] Terms match visible routes and tested state transitions; pending/future items are not shipped as live.
+- [ ] Any proposed canonical changes have owner decision and traceability.
+
+### 16.4 Change log
+
+| Date | Version | Change | Authority |
+|---|---|---|---|
+| 2026-10-17 | 1.0.0-candidate | Initial source-grounded language contract and copy audit for owner review | Analyst proposal; **not adopted** |
+| 2026-10-19 | 1.0.1-candidate | Correct LANG-P15 merged-file presence; original audit baseline and terminology preserved | Codex Task0024 factual maintenance under owner mandate; proposal status and owner adoption/merge gates unchanged |
+| 2026-10-20 | 1.0.2-candidate | Link scoped delegated language dispositions; clarify existing Library creation copy while retaining the proposed contract and historical question rows | Codex Task0035 under one-time owner delegation, 19 October 2026; external factual/security gates remain |
+
+## 17. Audit of actual repository language
+
+This audit is a research finding, not an instruction to change code immediately. Path-level observations are from default-branch source at `f371448`. Suggested replacements are `PROPOSED_LABEL` unless already present.
+
+| Severity | Location | Current text / pattern | Proposed treatment | Evidence and confidence |
+|---|---|---|---|---|
+| **Critical** | `apps/web/src/app/books/page.tsx`, `book-library-state.ts` | **Save again anyway** after uncertain book create | Preserve unknown-ACK semantics; add **Check your books** first and a clear duplicate-risk confirmation before **Create book again** | High: exact current code; backend may have committed |
+| **High** | `apps/web/src/lib/library/book-library-state.ts` | **Could not save this book** vs form **Create** and "save result unknown" | Prefer **create book** consistently for POST creation; keep distinct definite/unknown outcomes | High: exact current strings and backend POST |
+| **High** | `apps/web/src/app/login/page.tsx` | **Account requested. Check your email if confirmation is enabled.** | After inspecting actual Supabase confirmation and response behavior, show precise **Check your email to confirm your account** *only if applicable*, or verified alternative. Avoid conditional configuration jargon | High: current string; actual deployment setting unknown |
+| **High** | `apps/web/src/app/login/page.tsx` | Raw `error.message` from provider displayed | Map known auth failures to clear secure copy; protect sensitive error details; keep fallback helpful | High: current source; individual provider messages uninspected |
+| **High** | `apps/web/src/app/api/books/route.ts` | API errors `unauthenticated`, `api_not_configured`, `invalid_document`, `acknowledgment_unknown`, `service_unavailable` | Keep internal error contracts; map to safe author messages in client, preserve exact semantics | High: code confirmed; not all errors leak directly today |
+| **High** | Planned manuscript editor (not built) | Draft statuses include **Saved**, **Saving**, unknown ACK, conflict | Require distinct author copy and tests; do not say **Saved** before confirmed server revision | High: accepted save principles; planned UX only |
+| **High** | Planned Story Profiles/AI review (not built) | "Accept", "Approve", "Canon", "Fact" across design drafts | Label prose decision **Accept suggestion** vs fact decision **Approve fact**; distinguish source-linked candidate | High semantic evidence; proposed exact labels |
+| **Medium** | `apps/web/src/app/books/page.tsx` | Form **New book title**, button **Create** | **Book title**, **Create book** | High: real UI text; copy recommendation contextual |
+| **Medium** | `apps/web/src/lib/library/book-library-state.ts` | **Please sign in to see your books.** | **Sign in to see your books.** | High: exact string; concise style proposal |
+| **Medium** | `apps/web/src/lib/library/book-library-state.ts` | **Unable to load books. Try again.** | **Couldn't load your books. Try again.** | High: exact string; style proposal, not functional defect |
+| **Medium** | `apps/web/src/app/books/page.tsx` | **Home** from book library | **Back to home** or context-verified destination label; do not rename blindly | High: exact route/text; moderate UX effect |
+| **Medium** | `apps/web/src/app/login/page.tsx` | Sign-in and account-creation controls share one password field with `autoComplete="current-password"` | Clarify any create-account vs sign-in guidance after checking actual auth implementation; separate UX states if approved | High: actual code; input-behavior issue adjacent to copy |
+| **Medium** | Design: PLAN-03, DES-002 | **Story Workspace**, **Story Profiles**, **Story Bible**, **Storyline/Thread** | Keep profiles as approved capability; Story Workspace as proposed shell; resolve primary label for thread vs storyline with author feedback | High: terms observed, approval boundaries distinct |
+| **Medium** | Wave 5 vs ADR-0007 | "134 logical aliases", "130 expanded defaults ON" vs unresolved source mapping/certification | Never advertise fully active certified defaults until approval/reproducible mapping evidence | High: document discrepancy; pending validation |
+| **Low** | `apps/web/src/lib/library/book-library-state.ts` | `Loading your books...`, `Signing out...` | Typography polish to **Loading your books.**, **Signing out.** | High: exact source; low impact |
+| **Low** | `/` homepage | **This is the first implementation foundation, not a public release.** | Keep on development environment; remove/rewrite only under separate production-launch task | High: accurate internal environment disclaimer |
+| **Positive** | `/books` and `/login` | **Your books**, **Sign in**, **Create account**, **Sign out** | Retain; familiar and unambiguous | High: exact source |
+| **Positive** | `LIBRARY_COPY.createUnknown` and `.createAbsent` | Explicit uncertainty and duplicate risk | Preserve semantic caution through any shorter language | High: correctness-positive current code |
+| **Positive** | `book-library-state.ts` phases | Loading / empty / populated / error / unauthorized separated | Maintain separate view states; don't show empty while loading/error | High: actual implementation |
+
+**Severity meaning:** Critical = a misleading action or status could cause incorrect/destructive author decisions; High = major trust/semantic or task confusion; Medium = discoverability/clarity; Low = polish. Planned missing terminology isn't a live deployed defect. Some high findings describe an implementation requirement rather than a current author-visible failure.
+
+## 18. Unresolved decisions - do not silently settle
+
+**Current scoped dispositions (20 October 2026):** [LANG-P01-P15](docs/reports/VEL-LANGUAGE-DISPOSITIONS-2026-10-20.md) records the ordinary terminology and existing Library copy selected under the one-time owner delegation. The table below preserves the original proposal questions as dated history. The whole language contract remains proposed-owner-review; scoped copy integration is not blanket approval, hosted acceptance or implementation of future capabilities.
+
+| ID | Question and evidence | Recommended next decision | Approval condition |
+|---|---|---|---|
+| `LANG-P01` | **Is the working product name Vellistry fully cleared?** Current brand in UI and repo; earlier market/name review was provisional. | Retain Vellistry in existing build; do not claim registered mark. | Owner legal/trademark/domain check before commercial brand claim. |
+| `LANG-P02` | **Which exact source-version mapping becomes installable default guidance?** ADR-0007 freezes compact+expanded variants, while Wave 5 specifies 134 logical aliases provisionally. | No public numeric "130/134 active" claim until certified mapping. | Owner approves versioned map and R01 validation. |
+| `LANG-P03` | **Should story navigation be "Story Workspace", "Story Profiles" or "Story Bible"?** Profile functionality accepted; design shell proposed; competitors commonly use Story Bible/Codex. | **Story Workspace** as shell and **Story Profiles** for relevant records; do not merge into Story Bible yet. | Owner UX naming decision plus real-author task tests. |
+| `LANG-P04` | **Storyline vs story thread:** approved MVP says Characters + Storylines/Threads, drafts vary. | **Story threads** for recurring plotline records; allow storyline in help. | Author testing, schema/interaction clarification and owner sign-off. |
+| `LANG-P05` | **Chapter vs scene as editing unit:** approved initial DB stores chapters; long-term vision includes scenes. | Keep **chapter** authoritative in current app. | Separate scoped scene model and design/owner approval. |
+| `LANG-P06` | **"Create book" unknown-save retry:** current `Save again anyway` can create duplicate; guard semantics deliberate. | **Check your books** first; add warned **Create book again** confirmation if supported. | Reviewed UX flow, tests for unknown ACK and duplicate prevention. |
+| `LANG-P07` | **Sign-up result:** current copy says confirmation email *if enabled*; hosted Auth setting unverified. | Use precise verified confirmation copy per configuration. | Hosted auth state and email confirmation test. |
+| `LANG-P08` | **What should AI settings promise about connection persistence?** Wave 5 selected session-only key; runtime not built. | **Use your own API key**; disclose session-only behavior, possible provider cost, actual recipient. | Authorized implementation and route/privacy/cost tests. |
+| `LANG-P09` | **How will "Undo" differ between editor keyboard undo and committed AI revision undo?** Revision transaction design selected; product UI not built. | **Undo** for immediate text edit; **Undo accepted change** for persisted reviewed AI edit if supported. | UI and transactional history implementation/approval. |
+| `LANG-P10` | **Which save/recovery statuses are guaranteed?** Current book-create status is not chapter autosave; save CAS planned. | Keep explicit **Unsaved**, **Saving**, **Saved**, **Save status unknown**, **Conflict**, **Recovered draft** conditioned on evidence. | Browser/database/local-recovery acceptance tests. |
+| `LANG-P11` | **What is included/retained on export, restore, deletion?** ADR-0004 defines target archive; no complete converter/restore verified. | Distinguish **Download manuscript** vs **Download book backup**. | Fidelity, restore, retention, privacy and consent evidence. |
+| `LANG-P12` | **Locale, English variant and translation strategy?** Current `<html lang="en">`; no localization system selected. | Use clear source English and locale-aware formatting; defer dialect/translation default. | Audience research, accessibility/IME tests, owner decision. |
+| `LANG-P13` | **What are the canonical account and nested-book settings screen labels/routes?** Current only `/`, `/login`, `/books`. | **Account settings**, **AI settings**, **Export and backup** as proposed labels. | Approved navigation design and actual routes. |
+| `LANG-P14` | **Which model/provider route statuses and cost figures may be displayed?** OpenRouter first adapter chosen; live real-model acceptance not completed. | Unavailable when not verified; label monetary values as estimates with units. | Real provider route/privacy/usage tests and approval. |
+| `LANG-P15` | **Whether the proposed LANGUAGE.md itself is effective:** `LANGUAGE.md` is present on merged main `fca0d96fd071f1165e3e16658d821a3d36a20293` via PR #80 (rechecked 2026-10-19); metadata remains `proposed-owner-review`. Repository presence is not owner approval of all15 decisions. | Adopt after owner review; keep `status: proposed-owner-review` meanwhile. | Owner approval and scoped documented repo integration. |

+### 19.3 UX/content and accessibility guidance
+
+- [Microsoft Writing Style Guide](https://learn.microsoft.com/style-guide/) - clear, direct product writing; [formatting UI instructions](https://learn.microsoft.com/style-guide/procedures-instructions/formatting-text-in-instructions).
+- [GOV.UK button guidance](https://design-system.service.gov.uk/components/button/) - verb/action labels, sentence case and explicit destructive actions.
+- [GOV.UK error messages](https://design-system.service.gov.uk/components/error-message/) - describe problem and next step without technical jargon.
+- [GOV.UK headings](https://design-system.service.gov.uk/styles/headings/) - sentence case and sensible hierarchy.
+- [W3C WAI labels](https://www.w3.org/WAI/tutorials/forms/labels/) - explicit control naming.
+- [W3C WAI form instructions](https://www.w3.org/WAI/tutorials/forms/instructions/) - placeholders do not substitute for visible labels.
+- [WCAG 2.2 understanding error identification](https://www.w3.org/WAI/WCAG22/Understanding/error-identification) - identify the affected field and problem in text.

+### 19.4 Research limitations and confidence
+
+- Direct repository read: **high confidence** for source-level current copy, routes, enums and governance at the stated SHA. This is **not** a live user browser test.
+- Figma Make/secondary prototypes: **reported or source-audited historical design evidence**, not independent interactive reevaluation on 2026-10-17. Direct Figma MCP metadata requests for the canonical Design file on 2026-10-17 were blocked by the connected account's Starter-plan tool-call limit; no new screen content was independently retrieved. The repository's Figma handoff, node references and prior walkthrough report were used instead.
+- Competitors: **current official docs** checked, not logged-in account walkthroughs; products can change and are not direct user-research validation.
+- Actual author vocabulary preference: **not validated** through interviews or task-based naming experiments; `PROPOSED_LABEL` changes require that review where important.
+- Runtime hosted Supabase configuration, chapter save, real BYOK costs and AI output: **not verified** by this terminology audit.
+- Scope/approval remains governed by accepted project decisions; this candidate does not change the accepted product boundary or authorize code execution.

+
+# ADR-0008 - Codex-primary governed agentic implementation
+
+## Context and problem statement
+
+Vellistry uses Git and Markdown as the canonical engineering knowledge/control plane. The accepted project constitution separates evidence, design decisions, implementation and owner approval. The planned workflow had ChatGPT prepare bounded tasks and ContextPacks while **Cursor Agent** performed implementation; its outputs were independently reviewed and reconciled through GitHub PRs and CI.
+
+After PR #64 merged, direct GitHub editing by ChatGPT produced draft PR #65 for hosted Supabase preflight. The owner then reconfirmed that Vellistry follows an **agentic coding** workflow and explicitly said **"make it an adr we are using codex now"**. The agent responsible for normal implementation must therefore be updated without rewriting past Cursor execution history or implying any broader authorization.
+
+This is a **development operating model decision**, not a product stack, deployment, licensing, security-gate or production-readiness decision.
+
+## Decision
+
+**Codex is Vellistry's default primary code implementation agent starting 2026-10-14**, for each newly authorized bounded coding task. Preserve the existing role-based control plane:
+
+| Role | Responsibility | Authority boundary |
+|---|---|---|
+| **Project owner** | Select/approve scope, consequential changes, security posture, spending, database operations, PR acceptance/merge and release | Sole final decision maker; permitted to self-review under the constitution |
+| **ChatGPT - architect/coordinator** | Read current repository evidence and accepted ADRs; define requirements, task IDs, ContextPacks, acceptance/negative tests, path allowlists and exclusions; assess Codex reports, diff and CI; advise owner | Does **not** make owner decisions, treat its assessment as independent human review, or act as the default feature-coding executor |
+| **Codex - primary implementer** | On an explicitly scoped task, inspect pinned source, implement code/tests in an isolated Git branch/worktree, run valid checks, produce a factual ExecutionBundle and proposed PR | No self-authorization to change scope/ADRs, merge, deploy, perform cloud SQL/migrations/resets, incur paid costs, use real manuscripts or disclose secrets |
+| **GitHub - source of truth and review ledger** | Host code, authoritative docs, versioned ADRs, task/ContextPack/ExecutionBundle artifacts, PRs, CI, decision history and current state | PR creation and green CI do **not** equal owner acceptance or deployment readiness |
+| **Independent verifier (optional)** | Challenge the source diff, independently rerun tests and negative scenarios when possible | Advisory; the same agent's self-review is not an independent execution, and no second human is mandated |
+
+**Default work loop:**
+
+1. **Observe:** ChatGPT inspects the merged `main` SHA, current PRs, accepted requirements/ADRs and latest top of `CURRENT-STATE.md`; distinguish verified facts from older historical status.
+2. **Authorize a specific slice:** The owner approves the bounded objective and sensitive actions. ChatGPT prepares a unique task/ContextPack with source SHA, intended changes, allowed/forbidden paths, behavioral acceptance checks, security and cost restrictions. A prepared packet does not grant approval on its own.
+3. **Dispatch to Codex:** Codex executes in a dedicated branch/worktree against the designated SHA, without reusing old task/pack IDs or overwriting another agent's in-progress branch. Agent may use the existing repository tools/CI and must declare tool/model/runtime in its evidence.
+4. **Report evidence:** Codex supplies an ExecutionBundle with base/head SHA, changed paths, actual commands and exit codes, unit/integration/browser results with real environment and fixture descriptions, `PASS|FAIL|BLOCKED|NOT_RUN`, original artifacts and `FACT|INTERPRETATION|PROPOSAL`.
+5. **Review:** ChatGPT reviews the exact submitted code, contracts, tests, workflow behavior, known risks and evidence. The owner determines corrections, rejection or PR acceptance/merge. CI success alone cannot waive human acceptance/security/recovery testing.
+6. **Reconcile:** After an approved merge, verify files on merged `main` and post-merge checks; update `CURRENT-STATE.md` and issue/task status, preserving past decisions and results. Proceed to a new slice only after its own authorization.
+
+Codex is a **role assignment**, not a mandatory IDE or subscription. Cursor may continue as an editor, historical test environment or explicitly assigned alternate agent, but **Cursor Agent is no longer the default implementer**. Other agents may implement a task only when the owner explicitly reassigns that task; do not silently switch providers or claim that a ChatGPT-side GitHub change was executed by Codex.
+
+**Existing work:** PR #64 remains a valid merged Cursor-era implementation and its recorded verification evidence does not change. Draft [PR #65](https://github.com/Ben-001-sys/Vellistry/pull/65) is a **ChatGPT-authored, not Codex-executed** implementation proposal; do not relabel its origin, auto-merge it or treat its tests as Codex evidence. It may be reviewed, adopted via a fresh bounded Codex handoff, revised or closed by the owner, with its actual provenance preserved.
+
+**Security boundary:** The owner selected hosted **Supabase Cloud** project `vellistry-dev`, not a locally self-hosted Supabase installation. All hosted schema migrations, Auth fixture creation, remote resets, real manuscript use, deployment and paid provider calls remain subject to separate explicit task-level authorization and appropriate testing. In particular, the historical signed-out-but-still-valid JWT/direct PostgREST concern remains a private-pilot blocker until mitigated and verified.
+
+## Alternatives considered
+
+1. **Continue Cursor Agent as default:** keeps earlier environment but contradicts the latest explicit owner tool selection. Preserve Cursor-era history instead.
+2. **Let ChatGPT directly commit all feature changes through GitHub:** convenient for small edits but bypasses the chosen Codex execution role and muddies agent provenance. ChatGPT may maintain planning/decision records, not silently act as the default code implementer.
+3. **Allow unrestricted, interchangeable coding agents:** flexible but weakens permission isolation and reproducibility when task ownership changes implicitly.
+4. **Codex primary under the existing governed task/PR loop (selected):** retains traceability, single-task ownership and verifiable outputs while honoring the updated owner direction.
+
+## Rationale
+
+The owner's instruction identifies Codex as the current implementation tool. Keeping architecture, execution, independent review and approval responsibilities separate avoids silently treating agent-produced code, CI results or a prior conversation as accepted authority. The Git-centred knowledge and task packet approach permits future tool changes without changing the Next/Nest/Supabase/Tiptap architecture.
+
+No performance, reliability or cost superiority between Codex and Cursor is asserted. This choice is an **owner workflow preference and accountability decision**, not a benchmark result.
+
+## Consequences and trade-offs
+
+- Future default implementation prompts, agent handoff references and code-owner descriptions should name **Codex**. Older Cursor artifacts remain intact and labelled as historical; do not rewrite them as if Codex produced them.
+- Codex needs a verified workspace/Git access method and clear approval/tool permission boundaries for each task. The specific Codex interface (web, desktop, CLI or connected agent) is **not established by this ADR**.
+- ChatGPT should deliver **specification, acceptance tests, task packet, review and decision support**, rather than directly creating ordinary feature code/PRs without an explicit exception.
+- Existing ContextPack/ExecutionBundle schemas and Git/CI controls remain in force until independently amended and validated; do not claim automatic Codex integration if the current workflow does not support it.
+- The single-owner self-review policy remains effective. Independent agent review helps but cannot replace required owner approvals.
+- Migration, production/deployment, private manuscript, provider-spending, security and release gates are unchanged. No new subscriptions or paid API use is approved.
+
+## Evidence and verification limits
+
+The owner's direct 2026-10-14 message is the decision source; the conversation permalink has not been archived. It approves **the default implementer change and ADR record**, not a particular Codex runtime, verified Codex GitHub connection, successful task handoff or a security-gate transition. This documentation PR itself is **not Codex implementation evidence**.
+
+Record status is **accepted by the owner as a workflow decision**. Repository enforcement via docs and completed Codex task evidence are distinct, separately verifiable steps. +# IMP003D pinned implementation contract
+
+Native IndexedDB database v1, draft store keyed by [author,book,chapter,client]. Record schema1 includes baseline, exact validated Tiptap JSON, pending immutable operation, monotonic record sequence, timestamp. Async load/write/delete settle only on transaction complete, CAS in one readwrite transaction. Mismatch never silently overwrites. Unavailable/blocked/quota/abort/corrupt/incompatible explicit failure states. No deletion on logout. No default identity or persistent credentials.
+
+Compose existing synchronous recovery ONLY as an explicitly in-memory working copy; async adapter owns durable evidence separately. Queue snapshots; generation + owner revalidation prevent stale publication. Saved on this device only when latest sequence has committed, separate from remote receipt; unknown remote retains original ID. Baseline mismatch blocks restoration; accepted document never auto replaced. Multi-tab optimistic conflict pauses persistence until explicit selection/rebind/discard, retaining in-memory writing. Explicit local discard CAS cannot delete a competing record. Test-only preview shows storage loss/shared-profile/no backup notice. No production imports.
+
+Unit tests validate strict schema/oversize/identity/pending and injected storage failures. Actual Chromium disposable-profile loopback fixture uses existing Tiptap components, bundle from existing esbuild tooling (no dependency change). Real reload/new tab/shared profile + commit, corruption, CAS competition, account/chapter switch, delayed/abort/quota failures and cleanup. Browser failure injection is labeled synthetic; native successful transactions/reload are real. Never claim quota disk exhaustion actually induced. Keep historical statuses; ready CI uses existing mandatory workspace suite; browser execution recorded separately without CI-policy change.
+
+Language source: LANGUAGE.md at reviewed basee6242df; blob 1b8a700218c8f93bfb4ca7972b90c00d03c673c0. Full source read separately; exceeds pack35000byte source limit. Applicable requirements: distinguish local draft vs accepted revision; no Saved without authoritative server ACK, Saved on this device only after completed local transaction; accurate unknown/failure/recovery labels and storage-loss/shared-profile notice. No canonical language policy edits.

+ }
+ async function still(epoch:number,s:CompositionScope){

+  const phase=d.phase==='unsaved'?'editing':d.phase==='unverified'?'unverified':d.phase==='recoverable'?'recovery':d.phase;
+  const copy:Record<string,string>={editing:'Local changes. No confirmed test revision yet.',confirmed:`Confirmed revision ${d.base} in this test environment only.`,unknown:'Save outcome unknown. + function parseReceipt(raw:unknown,op:SaveOperation){

+/** Deterministic synthetic recovery. Storage is injected; no browser/device durability claim. */
+export type DraftScope=Readonly<{author:string;book:string;chapter:string}>;
+export type DraftPhase="unsaved"|"saving"|"confirmed"|"rejected"|"conflicted"|"unknown"|"recoverable"|"unverified";
+export type DraftStorage={get(key:string):unknown;set(key:string,value:unknown):void};
+type Pending={id:string;text:string;base:number};
+type Entry={scope:DraftScope;text:string;base:number;phase:DraftPhase;pending?:Pending};
+export function createDraftRecovery(storage:DraftStorage,validate:(value:unknown)=>unknown,clientId:string){
+ if(typeof clientId!=="string"||!clientId||clientId.length>100)throw new Error("Explicit synthetic client identity required");
+ let active:Entry|null=null;let epoch=0;
+ const key=(s:DraftScope)=>JSON.stringify([s.author,s.book,s.chapter,clientId]);
+ function checked(s:DraftScope){if(!s||Object.keys(s).sort().join()!=="author,book,chapter"||Object.values(s).some(v=>typeof v!=="string"||!v||v.length>100))throw new Error("Invalid scope");return Object.freeze({...s});}
+ function persist(){if(active)storage.set(key(active.scope),JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(active)));}
+ function snapshot(){return active?JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(active)) as Entry:null;}
+ return {
+  snapshot,
+  pendingTicket(){return active?.pending?Object.freeze({epoch,scope:active.scope,id:active.pending.id}):null;},
+  bind(scope:DraftScope|null,accepted:unknown,revision:number){epoch++;active=null;if(!scope)return;const s=checked(scope);if(!Number.isSafeInteger(revision)||revision<1)throw new Error("Invalid revision");const text=JSON.stringify(validate(accepted));active={scope:s,text,base:revision,phase:"confirmed"};},
+  pause(){epoch++;if(active)active.phase="unverified";active=null;},
+  edit(value:unknown){if(!active)throw new Error("Unverified identity");active.text=JSON.stringify(validate(value));active.phase=active.pending?"unknown":"unsaved";persist();},
+  begin(id:string){if(!active||active.pending||active.phase!=="unsaved"||typeof id!=="string"||!id||id.length>100)throw new Error("Save unavailable");active.pending={id,text:active.text,base:active.base};active.phase="saving";persist();return Object.freeze({epoch,scope:active.scope,...active.pending});},
+  settle(ticket:{epoch:number;scope:DraftScope;id:string},outcome:"confirmed"|"rejected"|"conflicted"|"unknown",revision?:number){
+   if(!active||ticket.epoch!==epoch||key(ticket.scope)!==key(active.scope)||active.pending?.id!==ticket.id)return false;
+   const pending=active.pending;
+   if(outcome==="confirmed"&&revision===pending.base+1){const stale=revision<active.base;active.base=Math.max(active.base,revision);active.phase=stale?"conflicted":active.text===pending.text?"confirmed":"unsaved";delete active.pending;}
+   else if(outcome==="rejected"||outcome==="conflicted"){active.phase=outcome;delete active.pending;}
+   else active.phase="unknown";persist();return true;
+  },
+  recovery(){if(!active)return null;try{const candidate=storage.get(key(active.scope)) as Entry;if(!candidate||key(checked(candidate.scope))!==key(active.scope)||!Number.isSafeInteger(candidate.base)||candidate.base<1||typeof candidate.text!=="string"||candidate.text.length>300000)return null;validate(JSON.parse(candidate.text));if(candidate.pending){if(typeof candidate.pending.id!=="string"||!candidate.pending.id||candidate.pending.id.length>100||(!Number.isSafeInteger(candidate.pending.base)||candidate.pending.base<1||candidate.pending.base>candidate.base)) return null;validate(JSON.parse(candidate.pending.text));}return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify({...candidate,phase:"recoverable"}));}catch{return null;}},
+  recover(candidate:unknown){if(!active)throw new Error("Identity unavailable");const actual=this.recovery();if(!actual||JSON.stringify(actual)!==JSON.stringify(candidate))throw new Error("Invalid recovery candidate");active.text=actual.text;active.pending=actual.pending;active.phase=actual.pending?"unknown":"unsaved";/* retain current accepted base; never auto-save recovered content */persist();},
+ };

+
+  1. **The author is in control.** Do not imply the application or AI independently authors, accepts, publishes, or 
+establishes story truth.
+  2. **Use familiar writing words.** Prefer **book**, **chapter**, **manuscript**, **character**, **story thread**, 
+and **writing rule** in general explanations; reserve **Story Profiles** and **Rules Studio** for the named Vellistry 
+surfaces.
+> 3. **Never collapse these boundaries:** manuscript / outline / notes; draft / saved revision; AI suggestion / 
+accepted prose; suggested fact / approved story fact; AI prose acceptance / fact approval; local recovery / confirmed 
+online save; exported document / full book backup.
+  4. **Actions name their effect:** **Create book**, **Add chapter**, **Continue writing**, **Rewrite selection**, 
+**Accept suggestion**, **Reject suggestion**, **Approve fact**, **Dismiss suggestion**, **Download manuscript**, 
+**Download book backup**, **Sign out**. Display-name choices identified as `PROPOSED_LABEL` need owner review before 
+deployment.
+> 5. **Status is evidence, not decoration.** Never display **Saved** without a server durability acknowledgement or 
+**Saved on this device** without confirmed local persistence. A lost acknowledgement is **Save status unknown**, not 
+necessarily **Save failed** or **Conflict**.
+  6. **AI is optional.** Never require an AI key for ordinary book writing, manual Story Profiles, rule editing, or 
+supported export. Do not imply AI is free or that Vellistry pays provider bills.
+  7. **Current shipping boundary:** live implementation is login and a basic book library. Described 
+chapter/editor/rules/AI UI is mainly approved product scope plus proposed UX, not verified deployed behavior.
+  | `INTERNAL_ONLY` | Engineering/process name, not a suitable general UI label | May remain unchanged in code, data, 
+logs and engineering docs |
+  
+> Use `status` **per term** and, for high-impact changes, **per specific UI label**. A product concept's approval does 
+not make every draft string official.
+  
+  ## 2. Product context and audiences
+  **Primary role:** **Author** - a signed-in person who owns and edits their own books, rules and approved story 
+information. In user-facing first-person/system copy prefer **you**, **your books**, **your manuscript** over 
+repeatedly saying "the author." The existing system has one relevant product role. There is no approved collaborator, 
+editor, publisher, staff, moderator or administrator role model for the MVP. Do not invent corresponding role 
+switchers or access claims.
+  
+> **Intended writers:** novelists, serialized/web-fiction writers, and writers in many genres. Expect users who know 
+ordinary concepts such as books, chapters, drafts and characters, not necessarily APIs, tokens, source revisions, 
+database row-level security or model context-window mechanics. Some writers avoid AI deliberately; this is a supported 
+experience, not an onboarding failure.
+  
+  **Experience constraints:** desktop-first authoring with responsive consideration; keyboard, screen reader, IME and 
+RTL behavior require actual testing. Product English is the current source language (`<html lang="en">` in 
+`apps/web/src/app/layout.tsx`); no locale rollout or Ghana-only formatting has been approved. Treat region, spelling 
+preferences and date/currency format as localization decisions, not identity-based assumptions.
+  3. **Do not merge neighboring concepts.** Book vs series; chapter vs scene; notes vs manuscript; outline vs accepted 
+prose; rule vs canon; suggestion vs revision; connection vs selected model; download vs backup.
+  4. **Use specific action verbs.** "Create book" rather than "Create" outside an obvious create-book form. "Approve 
+fact" rather than "Accept" when the choice changes approved story information.
+> 5. **Show provenance and degree of certainty.** A proposed fact is **Suggested fact**, not **Fact added**; a saved 
+draft needs an actual receipt.
+  6. **Preserve creative agency and style.** Language should explain choices, not score an author's prose as 
+objectively "good/bad" or claim to detect AI-written text.
+> 7. **Do not blame authors.** State the problem and practical recovery action; never imply a session/token/storage 
+failure is their fault.
+> 8. **No false reassurance.** Never say *permanently saved*, *fully secure*, *guaranteed original*, *always private*, 
+*completely offline*, *lossless DOCX*, *zero cost*, or *immediately revoked* without specific verified guarantees.
+  9. **Don't promise availability.** An approved/future feature is not an active control until implemented, tested and 
+authorized for its environment.
+  10. **Write for accessible interaction.** A meaningful visible label and matching accessible name must work without 
+relying only on color, icon, placeholder, tooltip or pointer location.
+  | **Word count** | Number of words using the implementation's defined counting behavior | `1,234 words` as displayed 
+value | Estimated pages or reading time | `APPROVED_CONCEPT`; counting rules to test |
+  | **Focus mode** | Reduced-distraction manual writing view | Distraction-free writing in help | Full-screen alone, 
+read-only preview | `APPROVED_CONCEPT`; exact UI `PROPOSED_LABEL` |
+> | **Revision** | Persisted accepted version of a chapter/manuscript | **Version** in simpler explanations, if 
+clearly a saved snapshot | Unsaved browser draft; AI suggestion; backup | `APPROVED_CONCEPT` |
+> | **Revision history** | Author-visible record of saved accepted revisions, subject to implemented retention | 
+**History** within manuscript only | Browser Undo stack; generated proposal list | `APPROVED_CONCEPT`; 
+`PROPOSED_LABEL` |
+> | **Recovered draft** | Local/recoverable writing not automatically accepted as newest server revision | Local draft 
+if provenance requires it | Restored accepted revision; approved story fact | `APPROVED_CONCEPT`; `PROPOSED_LABEL` |
+  
+  ### 4.2 Story knowledge and author approval
+  | **Story Workspace** | Proposed larger view housing profiles, threads and future supporting material | **Story** 
+only if navigation context makes it clear | Treating a proposed view as an existing route | `PROPOSED_LABEL`; 
+Figma/design terminology |
+  | **Character** | Person/being within a given book, with identity and changing state | Character profile when 
+viewing its record | User account, role, actor, narrator | `APPROVED_CONCEPT` |
+> | **Character profile** | Author-maintained facts and evolving state for one character | Character in a clearly 
+titled Characters view | Full accepted manuscript, AI character draft | `APPROVED_CONCEPT` |
+  | **Story thread** | Continuing plotline, obligation or subplot whose development may change | **Thread** in its own 
+list | Chat thread, discussion thread; unrelated issue tracker | `PROPOSED_LABEL` based on approved Storyline/Thread 
+concept |
+  | **Storyline** | Descriptive prose synonym for plot development | Story thread only when describing same supported 
+record | Automatic alias for exact `thread` data record | `APPROVED_CONCEPT` semantically; nav mapping 
+`PENDING_DECISION` |
+  | **Applied guidance** | Applicable enabled subset for the current writing request | **What the AI will receive** if 
+preview shows complete outbound context | Proof the model obeyed the guidance | `PROPOSED_LABEL` |
+  | **AI assistance** | Optional model-assisted suggestions within writing workflow | **AI** in concise navigation 
+where clarity survives | Mandatory authoring engine | `APPROVED_CONCEPT` |
+> | **AI connection** | Configured access route using an author's API credential | Connect AI in action copy | Model 
+itself; permanently saved key | `PROPOSED_LABEL` for approved BYOK behavior |
+  | **AI provider** | External service handling model requests, initially selected OpenRouter route | Provider in 
+advanced settings | Vellistry itself as model trainer | `APPROVED_CONCEPT` |
+  | **Model** | Selected downstream model available via authorized provider route | AI model when first introduced | 
+Provider or API key | `APPROVED_CONCEPT` |
+  | **Continue writing** | AI proposes plain text at valid author-selected collapsed caret | **Continue** in an editor 
+menu with known context | Add to chapter end; automatic writing | `APPROVED_CONCEPT`; display form `PROPOSED_LABEL` |
+  | **Rewrite selection** | AI proposes alternate text for one contiguous selected span within one paragraph | 
+**Rewrite** when one span is already selected | Rewrite entire chapter/book; accepting immediately | 
+`APPROVED_CONCEPT`; display form `PROPOSED_LABEL` |
+> | **AI suggestion** | Unaccepted model-proposed alternative, held separately from saved prose | **Suggestion** | 
+Saved revision; direct accepted text; confirmed fact | `APPROVED_CONCEPT`; `PROPOSED_LABEL` |
+  | **Review suggestion** | Compare proposed prose against the source and choose a disposition | Review | Apply 
+automatically | `PROPOSED_LABEL` |
+  | **Accept suggestion** | Author decision to commit reviewed AI prose, subject to revision validation | **Accept** 
+inside unambiguous suggestion card | Approve fact; silently replace source | `APPROVED_CONCEPT`; display form 
+`PROPOSED_LABEL` |
+  | **Dismiss suggestion** | Decline a candidate fact/change without establishing it | **Dismiss** in fact-review 
+context | Delete already approved fact, reject prose | `PROPOSED_LABEL` |
+  | **Undo accepted change** | Compensating revision to reverse eligible last accepted proposal | **Undo** where 
+affordance is clear | Delete arbitrary later edits | `APPROVED_CONCEPT`; `PROPOSED_LABEL` |
+> | **Outdated suggestion** | Proposal pinned to earlier text/selection and no longer safe to accept | **Needs a new 
+suggestion** in recovery CTA | Save conflict, rule disabled | `PROPOSED_LABEL` |
+  
+  ### 4.4 Portability, privacy and account
+  | **File format** | The kind of downloaded/imported document (TXT/DOCX) | Publishing layout specification | 
+`PROPOSED_LABEL` |
+  | **Download book backup** | Export versioned portable archive retaining selected book text, approved profiles and 
+rule settings | Not "Export DOCX"; not proven cloud backup | `APPROVED_CONCEPT`; `PROPOSED_LABEL` |
+> | **Restore from backup** | Stage, inspect and explicitly approve recovery of archived state | Not import an 
+arbitrary DOCX, not overwrite automatically | `APPROVED_CONCEPT`; `PROPOSED_LABEL` |
+> | **Delete book** | Permanently remove a book subject to actual retention/recovery policy | Remove from list, 
+archive, unpublish | `APPROVED_CONCEPT`; `PROPOSED_LABEL` |
+  | **Sign in / Sign out** | Current authentication UI actions | Log in/log out variants except quotations | 
+`EXISTING_UI` |
+  | **Create account** | Current sign-up action | Request access if registration actually produces account | 
+`EXISTING_UI` |
+  | M1 | Books and chapters | **Your books**  **Book workspace**, **Chapters** | Basic `/books` yes; chapter 
+workspace no |
+  | M2 | Manual manuscript editing | **Manuscript** / **Write** | No integrated editor in `main` |
+> | M3 | Draft durability and portability | **Revision history**, **Export and backup** | No full editor 
+revision/recovery UI |
+  | M4 | Live Story Profiles | **Story Workspace**  **Characters**, **Story threads**, **Suggested facts** | No |
+  | M5 | Simplified Rules Studio | **Rules Studio** | No |
+  | **Move / Reorder** | Change arrangement of existing objects | Change chapter identity | **Move chapter up**, 
+**Reorder chapters** |
+  | **Enable / Disable** | Set participation of a rule/feature, reversibly | Delete a rule or approve a fact | 
+**Disable rule** |
+> | **Restore** | Bring back previous saved state/default configuration | Enable a rule toggle alone (use Enable) | 
+**Restore default rules**, **Restore from backup** |
+  | **Approve** | Make source-linked story fact authoritative | Accept proposed manuscript text | **Approve fact** |
+  | **Accept** | Commit a reviewed AI prose suggestion | Turn an uncertain fact into canon | **Accept suggestion** |
+  | **Dismiss** | Close/decline a candidate fact or non-destructive notice | Erase accepted state | **Dismiss 
+suggested fact** |
+  | **Undo** | Reverse an eligible operation within explicitly supported revision semantics | Guaranteed reversal of 
+arbitrary later edits | **Undo accepted change** |
+> | **Download** | Produce a file on the author's device | Publish/distribute a book | **Download manuscript** |
+  | **Export** | General workflow for preparing document in a chosen format | Full backup unless specifically selected 
+| **Export manuscript** as screen heading |
+  | **Delete** | Permanent removal subject to documented policy | Hide, unlink, disable or discard an unaccepted 
+alternative | **Delete book** |
+  | **Remove** | Detach an association without deleting underlying source | Permanent destruction or default source 
+archive deletion | **Remove from [list]** only if non-destructive |
+> | **Cancel** | Stop uncommitted operation or close current dialog without change | Destroy draft edits unless 
+disclosure is explicit | **Cancel** |
+> | **Discard changes** | Intentionally throw away unsaved local edits | Cancel navigation preserving edits | 
+**Discard unsaved changes** |
+  | **Sign out** | End current app session in supported sense | Guarantee that every previously issued token 
+immediately invalidates | **Sign out** |
+  
+  - A destructive button names the entity: **Delete book**, not **Confirm**, **OK**, **Yes**, or **Proceed**.
+  - For data loss, show the target name and affected data classes, retention limitations and whether a backup is 
+advisable/available; never invent a deletion guarantee.
+> - Navigating away with uncommitted edits requires **Keep editing** / **Leave without saving** only when those 
+effects are accurate; offer recovery choice if the implementation supports it.
+  - Do not label a blind retry safe when a previous create could have succeeded. State duplicate risk *before* 
+presenting an action that might create another record.
+> - Only use **Undo** after actual revision receipt and within applicable safety rules. If not reversible, say why and 
+offer appropriate review/recovery, not a fake Undo.
+  
+  ## 9. Forms, search, tables and data presentation
+  | `unauthorized` | **Sign in to see your books.** | Current session is not authenticated | Your books were deleted |
+  | `error` | **Couldn't load your books. Try again.** | Failed authorized load; no false list | No books yet |
+> | `createPending` | **Creating book.** | Create request actually in progress | Saved |
+> | `createError` | **Couldn't create this book. The title is still here.** | Definite pre-forward or other confirmed 
+failure and draft preserved | Book lost; retry definitely safe if uncertain |
+  | `createUnknown` / `acknowledgment_unknown` | **We couldn't confirm whether the book was created. Check your books 
+before trying again.** | Server acknowledgement genuinely unknown | Create failed; Book created |
+  | `createAbsent` | **We still can't confirm this book was created. Creating it again could make a duplicate.** | 
+Refreshed list didn't prove the write; risk persists | Definitely absent / safe to retry |
+> | `session_changed` | **Your session changed. This book wasn't saved to this account.** | Verified identity change, 
+not a generic timeout | Account data exposed |
+  | `signingOut` | **Signing out.** | Sign-out started | Signed out |
+  | `signOutError` | **Couldn't complete sign-out. You may still be signed in. Try again.** | Sign-out failure 
+observed | Signed out everywhere |
+  The current exact `LIBRARY_COPY` strings in `apps/web/src/lib/library/book-library-state.ts` use a combination of 
+"save" and "create" language. Proposed revisions above are **not permission to bypass the existing unknown-ack safety 
+logic**. Prefer *create* for book creation as long as the implementation still performs POST creation. The 
+intentionally cautious uncertainty semantics must be preserved.
+  
+> ### 10.2 Chapter manuscript save and recovery - approved intent, proposed UI
+  
+  | Underlying condition | Author-visible status | Necessary behavior |
+  |---|---|---|
+> | No local changes since last confirmed persisted revision | **Saved** or **Saved � revision [n]** | Actual 
+acknowledged durable server revision |
+> | Locally changed, not yet acknowledged | **Unsaved changes** | Never imply durability |
+  | Save request pending | **Saving.** | Actual request pending; keep editor responsive |
+> | Connectivity unavailable | **Offline** | Add **Saved on this device for recovery** *only* if IndexedDB local 
+persistence was confirmed; otherwise disclose not yet safely stored |
+  | Request outcome unknown; acknowledgement possibly lost | **Checking save status.** or **Save status unknown** | 
+Reconcile before risk of duplicate write; not falsely a 409 conflict |
+> | Proven concurrent different revision | **A newer saved version exists. Review both versions.** | Non-destructive 
+compare/reconcile |
+> | Local recovery record found | **Recovered draft available** | Compare and explicitly choose, not automatic server 
+replacement |
+> | Save definitely failed | **Couldn't save your changes. Your draft is still available [only if verified].** | 
+Specific recovery action and no false local guarantee |
+  
+> Do not show **All changes saved** while local edits remain unacknowledged. Never use **Synced** unless actual 
+cross-device state is proven. Status is per affected chapter/book, not one blanket label copied from a different 
+record.
+  
+  ### 10.3 AI suggestion lifecycle - approved semantics, future interface
+  ### 11.1 Message composition rules
+  
+> Use **what happened  impact  next action** when helpful. Prefer a one-sentence message for routine results. Use a 
+second sentence for the next step or meaningful consequence. Do not expose JWT, RLS, CAS, PostgREST, HTTP 409, JSONB, 
+service-role, RPC, SQL or Supabase internals in ordinary author copy. Errors do not need apologies or jokes; don't 
+blame the author or promise data recovery without evidence.
+  
+> Successful mutations: speak only after a real confirmation. When an operation is still waiting, use 
+present-participle status (*Saving.*), never completed tense (*Saved*). When ambiguous, explicitly say **we couldn't 
+confirm**.
+  
+  ### 11.2 Specific scenarios
+  | No results after search | **No matching books. Try another search or clear filters.** | "No books yet." - wrong 
+condition |
+  | Session missing | **Sign in to see your books.** | "Unauthorized 401." |
+> | Session changed during create | **Your session changed. This book wasn't saved to this account.** | "Account not 
+found." |
+  | Sign-out failure | **Couldn't complete sign-out. You may still be signed in. Try again.** | "Signed out." |
+> | Unsaved manuscript edits | **Unsaved changes** | "Saved" without receipt |
+> | Confirmed manuscript save | **Saved** / **Saved � revision 12** | "Saved to cloud" without known server/retention |
+> | Save acknowledgement unknown | **We couldn't confirm the last save. Check the saved version before retrying.** | 
+"Network conflict", "Saved", "Save failed" |
+> | New server revision | **A newer saved version exists. Review both versions before deciding what to keep.** | 
+"Merge error 409." |
+> | Local draft found | **Recovered draft available. Compare it with the saved version.** | "Restored automatically." |
+  | No optional AI key | **AI isn't connected. You can keep writing without it.** | "Complete setup to start writing." 
+|
+> | AI request transfer | **This request will send the selected text and the listed story context to [provider/model]. 
+Review before continuing.** | "Your writing stays entirely on your device." |
+  | Potential provider charges | **Your provider may charge for this request. The amount shown is an estimate, not a 
+guarantee.** | "Free AI" |
+  | AI running | **Generating suggestion.** | "Writing your book." - implies automatic authorship |
+  | AI output received | **Suggestion ready. Review it before accepting.** | "Chapter updated." before author choice |
+  | AI source stale | **Your writing changed while this suggestion was being prepared. Request a new suggestion to 
+avoid overwriting your edits.** | "Apply anyway." |
+> | AI request fails | **Couldn't generate a suggestion. Your saved manuscript hasn't changed.** if verified | "Try 
+again" without unaffected-data assurance |
+  | Fact extracted | **Suggested fact: [change]. Review the source before approving.** | "Canon updated." |
+  | Fact approved | **Fact approved. It will be used as approved story information where relevant.** only if effective 
+context confirmed | "AI knows the truth now." |
+  **Approve story fact**: title **Approve this story fact?**; body explains source passage, target character/thread 
+and story time; primary **Approve fact**, secondary **Keep for review** or **Cancel** depending on actual persisted 
+state. Prose must not be accepted by this action.
+  
+> **Overwrite-risk recovery**: title **A newer saved version exists**; show local/server changes and available 
+verified recovery actions such as **Keep both versions** and **Review differences**. Don't offer **Replace saved 
+version** without an explicit approved conflict resolution protocol.
+  
+  **Delete book**: title **Delete "[Book title]"?**; body enumerates manuscript/notes/approved profiles/rules/backup 
+implications from actual retention policy. Primary **Delete book**, secondary **Cancel**. Do not claim permanently 
+deleted from backups unless retention policy guarantees it.
+  ### 11.4 Notifications and empty states
+  
+> - Use toast for low-risk confirmations with a lasting result that the page also reflects. Don't hide major 
+recovery/data-loss outcomes in a disappearing toast.
+  - `role=status`/polite live region for ordinary progress; assertive alert only for important actionable 
+interruptions. Verify keyboard focus and screen-reader behavior. Do not announce every keystroke/autosave tick.
+  - Empty states state **why** a collection is empty when known and suggest a relevant next action. Differentiate no 
+items / no search match / no permission / unable to load.
+  | AI consent | Transparent, non-coercive | **Review what will be sent to AI.** |
+  
+> Do not write "Your novel is bad," "Fix your AI slop," "AI detected," "Guaranteed human-sounding prose," "Magically 
+perfected," or "The AI understands your entire world." The researched fiction lenses support context-dependent 
+diagnosis and legitimate artistic exceptions; no isolated style device proves AI authorship.
+  
+  ### 12.2 Grammar and microcopy conventions
+  - Prefer active verbs, positive specific instructions and natural contractions where appropriate: **Couldn't save 
+your changes.** Do not use jokes or decorative exclamation marks for errors.
+  - Buttons/short labels normally have **no terminal period**. Complete help/warning sentences end with periods. Use 
+one ellipsis glyph `.` for ongoing status, e.g. **Saving.**, not three periods; this is a proposed UI polish, not a 
+forced code migration.
+> - Sentence fragments are fine as statuses: **Unsaved changes**, **Review needed**. The grammar must not hide 
+uncertainty.
+  - Display acronym expansions at first encounter if the writer must understand them: **API key** can be introduced as 
+a provider access key; **DOCX** and **TXT** are recognizable filename formats with accessible descriptions. Keep 
+**BYOK**, **CAS**, **JWT**, **RLS**, **JSON**, **MCP**, **RPC** out of ordinary screens unless the context is 
+explicitly technical setup/help.
+  - Write interface instructions describing outcomes, not mechanics: **Choose a book and start writing** rather than 
+**Invoke the chapter-write operation**.
+  - Accessibility: all controls have visible labels or equivalent accessible names; error summaries point to affected 
+fields, feedback is textual and not color-only, headings form a logical hierarchy, focus returns appropriately after 
+dialogs, and live regions are measured rather than spammed.
+  - Icons need labels if their meaning is not determinable from context: **Show chapter list**, **Close Story 
+Workspace**, **More book options**. Tooltip cannot replace accessible name.
+> - Avoid emoji as a substitute for essential status. Do not rely on icons to convey whether an action is approved, 
+saved, disabled, stale or safe.
+  
+  ### 12.3 Language and localization boundaries
+  The historical 13-domain fiction framework distinguishes reusable diagnostic lenses from rigid prohibitions. A 
+writing rule's inclusion in a prompt is measurable; model obedience and literary merit are separate. A user disabling 
+a particular rule changes active instructions, not the immutable original archive or the probability the model may use 
+an overlapping literary idea. Do not promise impossible exclusion of all semantic overlaps.
+  
+> **Critical R01 source identity:** ADR-0007 accepts preserving 260 compact/expanded source records with identity 
+`(raw_source_sha256, variant, printed_code)`. Some apparent duplicate IDs, especially `AR-04` and `AR-09`, have 
+different meanings. A printed `AR-04` alone is not a safe operational rule ID. The Wave 5 draft mentions 134 logical 
+aliases, 130 enabled expanded defaults and 4 additional compact entries; ADR-0007 expressly says the deployable active 
+mapping remains subject to explicit owner review/certification. **Do not manufacture a resolved label catalog** or 
+claim complete default activation before verifying the actual approved mapping. Keep the frozen source unchanged.
+  
+  ### 13.3 AI ownership and request transparency
+  | **Create book** | Create / Submit | Describes object and effect |
+  | **Add chapter** | Add page | Chapter is a semantic narrative unit, not a print page |
+> | **Manuscript** | Story Bible for drafted prose | Keeps authored prose separate from reference data |
+> | **Planning notes** | Draft manuscript | Notes do not enter accepted prose automatically |
+  | **Story Profiles** | AI memory database | Author-facing concept vs implementation details |
+  | **Suggested fact** | Canon fact (before approval) | Authority requires author decision |
+  | **Rewrite selection** | Rewrite chapter | One selected same-paragraph span only |
+  | **Review suggestion** | Applied successfully (before author action) | AI response is provisional |
+> | **Unsaved changes** | Saved (when pending) | Must reflect durability evidence |
+  | **Save status unknown** | Conflict / Save failed | A lost ACK is not proof of either |
+  | **Download manuscript** | Download complete backup | Single document export does not preserve all app state |
+> | **Download book backup** | Publish book | Recovery archive is not distribution output |
+  | **Sign out** | Delete session everywhere | Current security model cannot promise instant global token invalidation 
+|
+  | **Possible continuity issue** | AI detected bad writing | Context, genre and author intent matter |
+  
+  - inventory literals in `apps/web/src` (JSX text, attributes, `aria-label`, errors and constants) and compare 
+against this vocabulary;
+> - flag known high-risk display phrases (`Saved` without receipt context; `Save failed` on unknown ACK; `Canon 
+updated` before approval; `free AI` with no verified basis);
+  - test consent disclosure and actual transmitted context, including disabled rule exclusion;
+  - test exact sign-in/library/empty/error states, retry uncertainty and duplicate risk;
+  
+  - Before any change to user-facing navigation, page names, buttons, forms, dialogs, status messages, errors, 
+tooltips, accessibility labels, onboarding, AI consent or export wording, read root `LANGUAGE.md` alongside the 
+relevant accepted product requirements and current implementation.
+> - Follow `LANGUAGE.md` for owner-approved product terminology. Reuse canonical names consistently while allowing 
+explicitly documented contextual short labels; preserve distinct concepts such as manuscript vs notes, AI suggestion 
+vs accepted revision, suggested fact vs approved canon, and local recovery vs confirmed save.
+  - Verify what the interface and backend actually do before using success, save, privacy, cost, cancellation, 
+deletion or provider-availability claims. Do not expose internal transport codes as author-facing text or mark 
+proposed/future features as implemented.
+  - If a needed term is absent, proposed, disputed or inconsistent with approved scope, record the current wording, 
+candidate, affected surfaces and supporting evidence for owner review. Do not silently make a new canonical decision 
+or rename unrelated concepts.
+  ### 16.1 Adoption
+  
+> `LANGUAGE.md` currently has **proposed-owner-review** status. For it to become an effective source of truth: project 
+owner reviews all `PENDING_DECISION` high-impact questions and accepts the vocabulary scope; implementation agent 
+integrates document through an authorized task/PR as required by `AGENTS.md`; owner approves any updates to 
+`AGENTS.md`; actual merged-main SHA and current state are reconciled. An unmerged local download or an AI-generated 
+draft is not owner acceptance.
+  
+  ### 16.2 Introducing or revising a term
+  - [ ] Book / chapter / scene / notes / manuscript / series semantics preserved.
+  - [ ] AI suggestion acceptance and story-fact approval cannot be mistaken for each other.
+> - [ ] Local drafts, server save, unknown acknowledgement and true conflict have distinct messages.
+  - [ ] No unreviewed rule source collision or false default-pack completeness claim.
+  - [ ] No implied mandatory AI/provider spend or secret-persistence claim.
+  - [ ] Forms have explicit labels, useful validation and accessible error feedback.
+> - [ ] Deletion, export, backup, recovery and Undo explain actual effects and limits.
+  - [ ] Terms match visible routes and tested state transitions; pending/future items are not shipped as live.
+  - [ ] Any proposed canonical changes have owner decision and traceability.
+  | **High** | `apps/web/src/app/login/page.tsx` | Raw `error.message` from provider displayed | Map known auth 
+failures to clear secure copy; protect sensitive error details; keep fallback helpful | High: current source; 
+individual provider messages uninspected |
+  | **High** | `apps/web/src/app/api/books/route.ts` | API errors `unauthenticated`, `api_not_configured`, 
+`invalid_document`, `acknowledgment_unknown`, `service_unavailable` | Keep internal error contracts; map to safe 
+author messages in client, preserve exact semantics | High: code confirmed; not all errors leak directly today |
+> | **High** | Planned manuscript editor (not built) | Draft statuses include **Saved**, **Saving**, unknown ACK, 
+conflict | Require distinct author copy and tests; do not say **Saved** before confirmed server revision | High: 
+accepted save principles; planned UX only |
+> | **High** | Planned Story Profiles/AI review (not built) | "Accept", "Approve", "Canon", "Fact" across design 
+drafts | Label prose decision **Accept suggestion** vs fact decision **Approve fact**; distinguish source-linked 
+candidate | High semantic evidence; proposed exact labels |
+  | **Medium** | `apps/web/src/app/books/page.tsx` | Form **New book title**, button **Create** | **Book title**, 
+**Create book** | High: real UI text; copy recommendation contextual |

+to see your books.** | High: exact string; concise style proposal |

+compact+expanded variants, while Wave 5 specifies 134 logical aliases provisionally. | No public numeric "130/134

+  | `LANG-P03` | **Should story navigation be "Story Workspace", "Story Profiles" or "Story Bible"?** Profile

+semantics deliberate. | **Check your books** first; add warned **Create book again** confirmation if supported. | 
+Reviewed UX flow, tests for unknown ACK and duplicate prevention. |
+  | `LANG-P08` | **What should AI settings promise about connection persistence?** Wave 5 selected session-only key; 
+runtime not built. | **Use your own API key**; disclose session-only behavior, possible provider cost, actual 
+recipient. | Authorized implementation and route/privacy/cost tests. |
+  | `LANG-P09` | **How will "Undo" differ between editor keyboard undo and committed AI revision undo?** Revision 
+transaction design selected; product UI not built. | **Undo** for immediate text edit; **Undo accepted change** for 
+persisted reviewed AI edit if supported. | UI and transactional history implementation/approval. |
+> | `LANG-P10` | **Which save/recovery statuses are guaranteed?** Current book-create status is not chapter autosave; 
+save CAS planned. | Keep explicit **Unsaved**, **Saving**, **Saved**, **Save status unknown**, **Conflict**, 
+**Recovered draft** conditioned on evidence. | Browser/database/local-recovery acceptance tests. |
+  | `LANG-P11` | **What is included/retained on export, restore, deletion?** ADR-0004 defines target archive; no 
+complete converter/restore verified. | Distinguish **Download manuscript** vs **Download book backup**. | Fidelity, 
+restore, retention, privacy and consent evidence. |
+  | `LANG-P12` | **Locale, English variant and translation strategy?** Current `<html lang="en">`; no localization 
+system selected. | Use clear source English and locale-aware formatting; defer dialect/translation default. | Audience 
+research, accessibility/IME tests, owner decision. |
